# Seed 68 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - a plain run; and seeds 65-67 were plain too: islands never ran (Iteration 464)

`seed 68 8h widths noevolved islands then experiment footprint.py ... then experiment review-checks.py spread` asked at
b75372ada (Iteration 458). The evolution 06:18:37; it evolved 7 h 23 min. 28,007 designs, generations 0-109: 22,167
alive, 5,840 screened (21.7%), none died.

**Islands never ran - in this run or in seeds 65-67.** Their evolve.logs hold only "generation N:" lines (196, 178, 139
and 109), never a ", island " line: next-run.sh parsed "islands" and then detached, re-running itself with the parsed
words carried in the environment - NEXT_RUN_NOEVOLVED, NEXT_RUN_WIDTHS - but no NEXT_RUN_ISLANDS, so the detached
chain called evolve.py without --islands. **Fixed (464)**: the word carried through the detach, shown in the chain's
lines, and seed-facts.py reports from the log whether islands ran. So "the islands trial" of seeds 65-67 compared plain
runs with a plain run: **seeds 64-68 are five replicates** - at generation 109 (the chain's spread): fastest 0.1197 to
0.1298 (8.4% apart), smallest 14,039 to 14,207 (1.2%); seed 64, the trial's yardstick, sat at the slow end, beside this
run. The gains credited to islands were the replicates' spread at the fast end - ~5% as seeds 60-62 already showed.

**Facts.** Its front: 125 designs, 55 with the JIT; fastest 0.130 (32 bits: 0.341), smallest 14,207 bytes. heldout
(sieve): Spearman 0.967, the fastest tenth 0.252, the pick's regret +5.9%. Fast-end optimism 1.075. No 32-bit failure.
I+: 32 front designs with it, all with the fuser (26%). The gene votes over seeds 66-68: candidates **rtfuse** and
**jitos** (12 of 12); jitret off the list (a keep vote). The footprint check: 0.612 at 100 words, 0.115 at 10,000 (the
16 fastest too close to rank). Its chain's remote checks, of seed 67's front: results/xcheck-seed68.md.

---
# Evolved VM designs

22167 designs evaluated, 22167 alive, 5840 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 048ba8a584, speed 0.136, binary + image 29,464, memory 188 KB - Q 0.228 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
